I wake up with this heavy, empty feeling, and it kind of follows me around. Like the studio lights are on, but… there’s no music in me.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that your mood is depressed most of the day, nearly every day?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think so. It’s been most of the day, most days. I wake up with that heavy, hollow feeling… like I said, like the studio lights are on, but there’s no music in me.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that you take less pleasure in things you usually enjoy, or that activities feel more like a chore than they used to?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. Some things feel harder to start, I guess, but I don’t know if it’s that I enjoy them less or I’m just… tired of everything.\n\nDoctor: Have you found that you don’t take pleasure in things you usually enjoy, or that even the things you normally like feel flat or empty?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… maybe a little, but I can’t really tell. I mean… I still do some of those things, but it’s harder to tell if I’m enjoying them less or if I’m just not really getting into anything right now.\n\nDoctor: Can you think of something you used to really enjoy, like a hobby or activity, and whether you still get the same pleasure from it as before?\n\nPatient: I guess… dancing, maybe.
